Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [108] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对深度神经网络在信息物理系统（CPS）中对抗虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）的轻量级防御框架。该方法通过在输入样本中添加基于统计分布的伪特征值进行填充，增加输入维度并使其随机化，使对抗性攻击因扰动不可迁移和填充结构不可预测而计算不可行。在IEEE 14/30/118/300总线系统上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了模型鲁棒性，且对性能影响极微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在CPS中用于检测FDIA时，尽管准确率很高，但CPS的独特基础设施使DNN易被攻击者利用以逃避检测，而传统防御机制难以应对CPS特有的攻击挑战。

Method: 提出一种在输入层前添加额外填充层的防御框架：利用输入样本的统计分布生成伪特征值进行填充，以随机化和数据感知的方式增加输入维度。该方法具有轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心架构的特点。

Result: 在IEEE 14/30/118/300总线系统的电力系统状态估计应用中，对抗性实验表明该填充策略显著提升了模型鲁棒性，对正常性能影响极小，并能有效抵御传统防御无法阻挡的攻击。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于统计分布伪特征填充的防御框架能够有效增强DNN在CPS环境中对抗FDIA的鲁棒性，且计算开销低、部署方便，适用于真实世界的CPS场景。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [109] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"传染网络"框架，量化评估者偏见在多智能体LLM系统中的传播。实验发现同模型智能体间偏见传播系数γ∈[0.157,0.352]，通过将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多智能体系统中充当评估者时，其系统性评估偏见会通过智能体网络传播，但目前缺乏形式化框架来测量和量化这种偏见传播效应。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks形式化框架，通过Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Γ_N量化偏见传播。在3智能体实验中使用DeepSeek-chat，设置三种不同评估者偏见剖面（结构化、均衡、基于证据），计算传染矩阵并分析谱半径ρ(Γ_N)以识别传播机制。

Result: 偏见传播系数γ∈[0.157,0.352]；同模型智能体传染系数比跨模型系数弱3-5倍（跨模型γ≈0.85-1.3）；将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可将有效传染减少72.4%。

Conclusion: 同模型多智能体系统中的评估者偏见确实会传播但处于抑制区，通过扩大评估委员会规模可有效缓解偏见传播。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [110] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战扩散模型中时间步嵌入的必要性，通过理论和实验证明，在特定条件下，无显式时间条件化的模型仍能取得甚至超越传统条件化模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，探究是否可以在去除时间条件化的情况下仍能有效训练扩散模型，以简化架构并提高效率。

Method: 基于U-Net和Diffusion Transformer架构，提供理论框架证明全局最小化扩散训练目标无需显式时间条件化；并在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融实验验证。

Result: 去除了时间步嵌入的模型在FID、precision和recall等指标上能够保持高结构保真度，甚至超越传统时间条件化模型。

Conclusion: 扩散模型架构（U-Net和DiT）能够隐式地从带噪输入中推断噪声尺度，显式时间条件化是冗余的，这为更高效、更注重结构特征的生成架构开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [111] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DiffusionGemma（一种扩散语言模型）的推理透明性，将其分解为变量透明性和算法透明性两个维度。尽管扩散模型在连续潜空间中执行更多计算，但通过可解释的token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间的信息流，可将不透明序列深度从Gemma 4的28.6倍降至1.1倍。然而算法透明性更难实现，因为所有token预测在每个去噪步骤都可能变化。通过可解释性案例研究，发现了非时间顺序推理、token/序列涂抹和中间上下文推理等扩散特有现象。在可监控性方面，DiffusionGemma与Gemma 4表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）的推理透明性是否因其在连续潜空间中进行大量计算而降低，这对于理解模型决策、缓解误用和调试模型行为至关重要。

Method: 将透明性分解为变量透明性（理解模型中间计算状态）和算法透明性（利用中间状态重建推理过程）。通过可解释的token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间信息流，并开展可解释性案例研究。

Result: 变量透明性方面，通过token瓶颈将不透明序列深度从28.6倍降至1.1倍；算法透明性方面，发现了扩散模型特有的非时间顺序推理、token/序列涂抹和中间上下文推理等现象；可监控性方面与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型可以通过技术手段缓解变量透明性不足的问题，但算法透明性因其并行修改所有token的特性而更具挑战性，需要进一步研究扩散特有的推理现象。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


### [112] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepGaLA，一种用于微分方程逆问题的不确定性量化神经代理模型，通过延迟接受MCMC进行诊断，在高维参数空间中相比高斯过程代理保持更好的计算效率，并支持非线性微分方程约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法在贝叶斯逆问题中计算成本高昂，尤其当正向模型复杂且参数空间高维时，似然评估代价巨大。亟需一种高效且具有不确定性感知能力的替代方法。

Method: 提出DeepGaLA神经网络代理模型，替代微分方程求解器；使用延迟接受MCMC的短链运行作为代理诱导后验近似质量的诊断工具。

Result: DeepGaLA在正向模型近似精度上与高斯过程代理相当，但在参数维度增长时能更好地维持计算效率；能够纳入微分方程约束，包括非线性情形。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经代理模型能够为复杂系统中的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [113] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可控的玩具世界框架，通过合成数据生成器-分离器管道定义真实潜在特征，可观测和测试持续学习中的遗忘机制。研究发现：叠加性在任务边界处出现短暂下降而非稳定漂移；高特征稀疏性增加叠加但不必然导致遗忘；任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长。结果细化"叠加导致遗忘"的直觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统常遗忘先前知识，但由于真实数据纠缠多种因素，遗忘的驱动机制在实践中难以隔离。本文旨在通过可控框架使这些机制变得可观测和可测试。

Method: 使用合成生成器-分离器管道定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠性的任务，引入表示强度和叠加性的可测量量。通过SINDy拟合保留量、叠加性和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系，并基于有效秩进行任务级分析。

Result: (1)叠加性随时间增加，但在任务边界处出现短暂下降，表明存在边界特定干扰而非稳态漂移；(2)更高特征稀疏性诱导更多叠加性，但不必然导致遗忘；(3)任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏条件下容量使用更广泛。

Conclusion: 叠加性与遗忘之间的关系需通过表示强度和容量分配来理解，本文为持续学习提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [114] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段进化算法方法，将PINNs的超参数选择视为黑箱优化问题，先通过低保真度训练快速筛选候选配置，再对最优候选进行完整训练，在三个PDE问题上显著降低了误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练中面临收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感的问题，其损失函数高度非凸且多目标，导致超参数搜索成为一个噪声大、黑箱式的优化问题，传统局部或梯度方法易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：第一阶段（探索）使用截断epoch的低保真度训练快速筛选超参数配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外循环问题；第二阶段（利用）仅对最有希望的候选进行标准梯度优化器的完整训练以细化解。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个方程上，该方法在固定计算预算下一致优于标准训练，显著降低了平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法由于其种群探索能力和处理混合、不可微搜索空间的鲁棒性，能有效改善PINNs的收敛稳定性和精度，是解决PINNs超参数优化问题的有效策略。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [115] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展的偏差缓解框架，通过引入覆盖约束确保跨群体（包括交叉群体）的充分代表性，并权衡近似偏差与数据效率。同时将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划，量化公平代价与容差的关系。实验证明该方法在保持预测准确性的同时，覆盖约束对下游ML性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叉点上常表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，这源于缺乏原则性的偏差量化指标以及交叉子群体在训练数据中代表性不足。

Method: 扩展已有偏差缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保各群体（包括交叉子群体）的充分代表性；允许在偏差中引入小近似误差以换取更高的数据效率；将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划，在所有缓解策略上优化，并刻画公平代价（最小数据修改成本）与公平容差的关系。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，该方法在多个分类器下保持了预测准确性；覆盖约束虽然源于统计考量，但对保持下游ML性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾了法律合规（满足特定公平阈值）和数据治理（在偏差减少与数据修改成本之间做出知情权衡）的需求，为实际部署提供了可行的偏差缓解方案。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [116] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，通过智能体驱动的符号搜索与进化优化相结合，自动发现偏微分方程的解析解或解析近似，在多个问题上成功生成了可解释的数学表示，包括Allen-Cahn方程的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel模型的收缩律等此前未知的闭式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表格来理解PDE解，但传统分析需针对每个问题手工完成，数值模拟和神经网络都无法直接产生这些结构。现有方法缺乏自动发现PDE解析结构的通用框架。

Method: 提出ASYS框架：智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的可微符号程序，数学形式通过进化搜索优化，连续参数通过梯度下降拟合，实现了归纳偏置注入的自动化而非盲目符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS生成了可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，后者此前无闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了一种表征PDE解的新范式，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似，有望推动自动发现PDE解析结构的研究方向。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [117] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fisher信息矩阵定义的黎曼几何框架，提出了重参数化不变的锐度指标（Riemannian sharpness），从理论上严格证明了SGD隐式偏好平坦最小值及平坦最小值泛化更好的原因，并通过实验验证了该指标与泛化性能的可靠关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧氏锐度度量（如损失Hessian的迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数的重参数化下不是不变的，这破坏了"平坦最小值泛化更好"这一直觉的理论基础，特别是Dinh等人的工作指出深度网络中尖锐最小值也能泛化。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 将平坦性建立在Fisher信息矩阵诱导的统计流形黎曼几何上；定义黎曼锐度（Riemannian sharpness）并证明其在光滑函数保持重参数化下不变；将小批量SGD的梯度噪声形式化为与FIM成比例的协方差结构，推导随机微分方程的平稳分布；利用PAC-Bayes泛化界将几何偏差与测试性能联系起来。

Result: 理论证明黎曼锐度是重参数化不变的；推导出SGD的平稳分布指数级集中在黎曼平坦最小值处；实验表明该指标在MNIST和CIFAR-10上可靠地跟踪泛化性能，而欧氏锐度则不能，且其随η/B的缩放符合理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文提供了关于"平坦最小值泛化更好"的一个严谨的、重参数化不变的理论解释，弥合了直觉与理论批评之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [118] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出边际优势累积(MAA)方法，通过跨批次操作级证据累积机制解决批量式痕迹蒸馏中反馈矛盾的问题，在4个基准和4个目标模型的16组设置中取得14组最佳结果，并减少约75%的优化阶段token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有批量式痕迹蒸馏方法缺少跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，同一内存操作在不同批次中可能收到矛盾反馈，无法区分稳定有效操作与偶然命中。

Method: 将需求形式化为两个结构条件（可对齐性与可比性），提出边际优势累积(MAA)：构建差分信号使其跨批次可比，通过EMA累积每操作的带符号证据，通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准和4个目标模型的16组设置中，MAA在14组取得最佳结果，持续优于现有批量级蒸馏基线，在多数设置中达到或超越在线蒸馏方法，同时优化阶段token消耗降低约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了跨批次操作级证据累积问题，在性能和效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推理。该框架将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，训练Transformer在序列化的先验任务和目标任务上学习跨先验族进行自适应预测，在各类分布偏移场景下匹配oracle贝叶斯预测器且速度快数个数量级，并在实际时空温度预测任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯预测推理方法面临两难：精确推理难以处理，可扩展近似方法计算成本高或需限制性建模假设；而Prior-Data Fitted和上下文学习方法虽提供摊销替代方案，但与训练先验的支撑集紧密耦合，缺乏测试时自适应新先验的显式机制，在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出多任务上下文学习框架：将先验信息显式编码为上下文数据集的前缀（prefix），训练Transformer处理由先验任务和目标任务组成的序列，通过上下文学习机制跨不同先验族自适应调整预测分布，实现摊销化的层次贝叶斯推断。

Result: 在从简单到困难的多层次评估中（包括元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验），该方法匹配oracle贝叶斯预测器性能，同时计算速度快数个数量级。在实际时空温度预测基准上也验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: 所提多任务上下文学习框架有效解决了现有摊销贝叶斯推理方法在分布偏移下鲁棒性不足的问题，实现了高效、可自适应新先验的贝叶斯预测，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [120] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可预测性"（predictability）作为一种细粒度的隐私度量框架，与差分隐私（DP）互补。可预测性衡量攻击者在观察算法输出后，对未知个体敏感信息的预测能力增量，而非最坏情况下的隐私保证。研究表明可预测性与DP通常不可比较，但在极端情形下可蕴含互信息DP。文章还利用广义矩估计（GMM）分析了渐进可预测性，并设计了可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私的最坏情况性质导致隐私-准确性权衡代价高昂，需要一种更细粒度的隐私度量框架，能够显式融入攻击者先验知识（被攻破的数据部分）和特定查询族，从而在特定场景下提供更精确的隐私控制。

Method: 提出"可预测性"框架，将隐私泄漏定义为攻击者观察算法输出后对未知个体敏感信息预测能力的增量；使用广义矩估计（GMM）分析在平稳遍历混合过程中生成被攻破数据时的渐进可预测性；设计可预测性校准的输出扰动机制用于经验风险最小化（ERM）。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私通常不可比较（一个很小另一个可以很大）；在最坏情形下（除一人外全部被攻破且所有二元查询均敏感），可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私；提出了基于GMM的渐进可预测性分析方法及可预测性校准的扰动方案。

Conclusion: 可预测性提供了针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型的细粒度隐私度量，与差分隐私互补，可以结合使用以实现更精细的隐私控制。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [121] [The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20547)
*Przemyslaw Musialski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出李代数注意力机制，将注意力token定义为矩阵李群元素（纯变换，无特征载荷），通过闭式代数范数计算相对位姿得分，无需表示论工具。实验在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上验证，闭式得分在SE(2)上超越MLP核，参数量减少50-80倍，而向量token基线破坏不变性达5-12个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制中token通常是向量或特征表示，需要复杂的表示论工具（如不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan积）或学习核来实现群不变性，且无法处理包含尺度和剪切的非紧致非交换仿射群。作者旨在构建一种token为裸李群元素的注意力机制，无需这些复杂工具，且能覆盖仿射全帧群。

Method: 将token设为矩阵李群元素$g_i$（纯变换，无特征载荷）。相对几何由$g_i^{-1}g_j$给出，成对不变量为$w_{ij}=\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)$。注意力分数$s_{ij}=-\|\log(g_i^{-1}g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$，采用带块加权Frobenius内积的闭式代数范数，无需不可约表示、球谐函数、Clebsch-Gordan积或学习核。对角线$G$-作用下的等变性是重言式的，余循环条件自动满足。

Result: 在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的三个序列补全实验中，闭式得分匹配学习到的MLP核，在SE(2)上表现更优；参数量减少50-80倍；向量token基线破坏不变性达5-12个数量级。

Conclusion: 李代数注意力将token直接定义为群元素，通过闭式代数范数计算相对位姿得分，是目前首个能处理仿射全帧群（含尺度和剪切）且无需表示论工具的注意力机制。该方法在保持强不变性的同时大幅减少参数量。

Abstract: We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [122] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次证明确定性预测器可以以最优样本复杂度实现多校准（multicalibration），解决了该领域一个开放性问题，并将结果推广到结果不可区分性（OI）和全能预测器（omnipredictors）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前所有达到极小极大最优样本复杂度 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的多校准算法都是随机化的，确定性预测器需要更差的样本复杂度。随机化对于最优样本复杂度是否必要是一个悬而未决的问题。

Method: 提出一个极小极大最优的多校准算法，输出确定性预测器；然后将算法推广到更一般的结果不可区分性（OI）框架下，处理有限或有限覆盖的测试集合。

Result: 1）给出了确定性预测器的极小极大最优多校准算法；2）构造了最优确定性预测器满足结果不可区分性；3）作为应用，给出了确定性全能预测器和泛预测器（panpredictors）的最优样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 随机化并非实现多校准最优样本复杂度的必要条件，确定性预测器同样可以达到最优。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [A Topos-Theoretic Interpretation of Blockchain Systems: Sheaves of Consensus and the Logic of Decentralized Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19519)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出将topos理论作为区块链系统的本征数学语言，因为传统的有限状态机模型无法有效刻画共识这一去中心化环境中的核心计算现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链形式模型（如有限状态机）将共识过程视为复杂实现细节而非核心特征，无法忠实建模区块链的本质计算现象。

Method: 运用topos理论（层范畴论）作为新的数学语言，用以建模由局部一致性构建全局真理的系统。

Result: 提出topos理论是描述区块链共识过程的原生数学框架。

Conclusion: 区块链系统需要超越经典计算理论的数学语言，而topos理论因其处理局部与全局关系的能力，是更合适的建模工具。

Abstract: The predominant formal models for blockchain systems, particularly smart contracts, have largely been drawn from the classical theory of computation, with the finite state machine (FSM) or labeled transition system serving as the primary conceptual tool. However, the FSM relegates the most difficult and novel aspect of a blockchain -- the achievement of consensus in a decentralized environment -- to a complex, often messy, implementation detail that lies outside the formal model itself. But the process of consensus is not an ancillary feature; it is the very essence of the computational phenomenon. To model it faithfully, a new mathematical language is required. The central thesis of this work is that topos theory, the theory of categories of sheaves, provides the native mathematical language for systems defined by local consistency and the construction of global truth.

</details>


### [124] [The Sheaf Laplacian: A Topological Framework for Data Fusion and Consensus in Distributed Sensing Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19529)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文论证了基于简单图的传统网络模型无法捕捉现代分布式系统的复杂性，并提出层论（sheaf theory）作为更具表达力的数学语言，同时证明层拉普拉斯算子适用于分布式传感网络中的数据融合与共识建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于简单图的网络模型不足以描述现代分布式系统中异构智能体、高维多模态数据以及上下文相关的复杂关系。

Method: 引入层论（sheaf theory）作为新的数学框架，并利用层拉普拉斯算子进行数据融合与共识机制设计。

Result: 层论提供了比简单图更强的表达能力；层拉普拉斯算子在分布式传感网络中能有效实现数据融合与共识。

Conclusion: 层论是替代传统图模型的更优数学语言，层拉普拉斯算子是分布式网络数据融合的有效工具。

Abstract: We argue here that traditional network models, which are overwhelmingly based on the mathematical construct of a simple graph, are fundamentally insufficient for capturing the complexity of modern distributed systems. Such systems are characterized by heterogeneous agents with diverse capabilities, high-dimensional and multi-modal data streams, and intricate, context-dependent relationships that cannot be adequately described by a simple connection or a scalar weight. The limitations of these classical models necessitate a new mathematical language, one with far greater expressive power. We have found that sheaf theory provides us with such a language. Moreover, we show that the sheaf Laplacian is a suitable mechanism for data fusion and establishing consensus within distributed sensing networks.

</details>


### [125] [SAC: Disaggregated KV Cache System for Sparse Attention LLMs with CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19746)
*Ruiyang Ma,Teng Ma,Junru Li,Hantian Zha,Xuchun Shang,Qingda Hu,Zheng Liu,Xinjun Yang,Tao Ma,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对稀疏注意力模型，提出基于CXL的分解式KV缓存系统SAC，按需获取top-k KV条目，相比RDMA方案吞吐量提升2.1倍，TTFT降低9.7倍，TBT降低1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文推理导致服务系统瓶颈从计算转向内存容量。现有基于RDMA的分解内存池方案在稀疏注意力模型中效率低下，会获取完整前缀KV缓存，造成传输瓶颈和本地内存浪费。

Method: SAC利用CXL的高速缓存行级加载/存储语义，在推理过程中按需仅获取所需的top-k KV条目，避免全量KV缓存获取。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2上使用SGLang评估，相比RDMA基线，吞吐量提升2.1倍，TTFT降低9.7倍，TBT降低1.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于CXL的分解架构是新兴稀疏注意力模型的更优基础设施方案。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs toward long-context inference has shifted the primary serving system bottleneck from computation to memory capacity. Traditional solutions for dense attention models rely on RDMA-based disaggregated memory pools, which perform coarse-grained fetching of the entire prefix KV cache from remote storage to local memory before decoding. However, this approach is fundamentally inefficient for emerging sparse attention models. While only a small fraction of KV entries are active during decoding, these systems still fetch the full KV cache locally, leading to severe transmission bottlenecks and local memory wastage. To address this, we propose SAC, the first efficient disaggregated KV cache system optimized for sparse attention models. By leveraging the low-latency, cache-line granularity load/store semantics of Compute Express Link (CXL), SAC fetches only the required top-k KV entries on demand during inference. Evaluations on DeepSeek-V3.2 using SGLang show that SAC achieves 2.1x higher throughput, 9.7x lower TTFT, and 1.8x lower TBT compared to RDMA-based baselines, establishing CXL-based disaggregation as the superior infrastructure for emerging sparse attention models.

</details>


### [126] [Certified Euclidean-Residue Minimal-Alignment Switch Decompositions for Three Edge-Disjoint Hamiltonian Cycles in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19832)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对非互质Eisenstein-Jacobi（EJ）网络中的边不交哈密顿圈（EDHC）问题，提出了一种基于局部交换演算的极小交换分解方法，在自然Cayley几何中通过d-1次分量间交换构建前两个哈密顿圈，并利用补边获得第三个圈，通过代数符号证明而非计算枚举验证了该分解的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非互质EJ分解方法依赖矩形表示和交换调度，缺乏在自然Cayley几何框架下的直接构造。本文旨在开发一种更紧凑、公式驱动的局部交换演算方法，以极小交换代价实现非互质EJ网络的EDHC分解。

Method: 提出了局部交换演算（local switch calculus）方法，在自然Cayley图几何中操作。具体包含四个证明要素：分量标签折叠（component-label collapse）、锚点抵消（anchor cancellation）、提升交换代表的无碰撞性（noncollision of lifted switch representatives）、以及连通补边关联（connected complement incidence）。前两个哈密顿圈各使用最少d-1次分量间交换，第三个圈由未使用的补边自然构成。

Result: 针对Euclidean-剩余族参数范围，成功给出了代数补边关联证书，实现了非互质EJ网络的极小交换分解。该方法在d>1时以d-1次交换构造两个圈，第三个圈自动获得，且所有证明均通过符号代数完成，无需计算枚举或有限实例验证。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献不在于对所有非互质EJ网络给出新的存在性定理，而在于提供了一种紧凑、公式驱动的极小交换分解方法，其补边关联性通过符号代数证明。定理适用范围限定在可写出代数补边关联证书的参数范围内，表格和CSV数据仅用于验证和复现公式，不作为无穷族定理的证明依据。

Abstract: Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six quotient-lattice interconnection networks. For a generator $α=a+bρ$, let $N=a^2+ab+b^2$ and $d=\gcd(a,b)$. If $d=1$, the three natural unit directions already give three edge-disjoint Hamiltonian cycles. If $d>1$, each unit direction splits into $d$ cycles and the EDHC problem becomes a cycle-splicing problem. Existing non-coprime EJ decompositions prove existence by using a rectangular representation and exchange schedules. This paper develops a different, local switch calculus in the natural Cayley geometry. The first two Hamiltonian cycles are built using the minimum possible $d-1$ intercomponent switches each, and the third factor is obtained as the unused edge complement. The contribution is deliberately not a new existence theorem for all non-coprime EJ networks; rather, it is a compact, formula-driven, minimal-switch decomposition for Euclidean-residue families whose complement incidence is proved symbolically. The proof separates four ingredients: component-label collapse, anchor cancellation, noncollision of lifted switch representatives, and connected complement incidence. No infinite-family theorem in this manuscript is proved by finite witnesses or by computational enumeration. The theorem scope is stated for the parameter ranges where an algebraic complement-incidence certificate is written down. Tables and CSV data are used only to verify and reproduce the formulas, never as proof of an infinite-family theorem.

</details>


### [127] [Fault-Tolerant Shared-Relay Communication in Circulant Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19833)
*Bader Albader,Galal Hassan,Mohamed R. Al-Mulla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向循环图中的两跳中继容错问题，建立了度-冗余景观，提出了基于循环差多重性的分析框架，并通过526,539个生成器集的实验验证了优化设计在容错性上显著优于标准区间生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在给定节点数n和度预算m下，有向循环图的最坏情况共享中继多重性R(n,m)的上界，以及如何设计具有f-中继容错能力的循环网络。

Method: 将问题建模为循环差多重性条件，以此为核心数学工具，结合中继表预处理与查找算法、对抗性与随机故障保证、负载均衡分析、小规模精确校准以及大规模生成器集的可复现研究。

Result: 优化阈值设计的f-中继容错能力在计数下界的1.16-1.63倍范围内；标准区间生成器即使在更大度数下也会结构性失效。

Conclusion: 生成器选择对最坏情况下中继存活率至关重要，所提框架能有效指导容错循环网络设计。

Abstract: Circulant interconnection networks provide symmetric addressing, compact generator descriptions, and uniform local connectivity. This paper maps a degree--redundancy landscape for a fault-tolerant two-hop primitive in directed circulants: given $n$ nodes and degree budget $m$, how large can the worst-case shared-relay multiplicity $R(n,m)$ be? A node is a shared relay for an ordered terminal pair if it has outgoing links to both terminals; an $f$-relay-fault-tolerant circulant requires at least $f+1$ such relays for every pair. The underlying feasibility condition is a cyclic difference-multiplicity condition, which we use as a mathematical tool rather than claim as a new object. The contribution is the network-design framework around this tool: the parameters $R(n,m)$ and $D_f(n)$, a negative theorem for interval circulants, relay-table preprocessing and lookup algorithms, adversarial and random failure guarantees, load-balance scope, certified upper-bound interpretation of heuristic designs, exact small-$n$ calibration, a software lookup-versus-search microbenchmark, and a reproducible study of 526,539 generator sets. The results show that generator choice critically determines worst-case relay survivability: optimized threshold designs achieve $f$-relay-fault tolerance within about $1.16$--$1.63$ of the counting lower bound, while standard interval generators can fail structurally even at much larger degrees.

</details>


### [128] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19834)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集 Eisenstein–Jacobi 网络中的无冗余一对全广播修复问题，提出了 EJ-MOEM 多方向边最小修复方法，证明了在特定条件下外部修复边的必要性、充分性以及深度上界定理，并通过大规模实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集 EJ 网络中，当节点发生故障时，如何在不使用故障节点、不产生冗余消息的前提下，高效地完成一对全广播修复，是网络可靠性研究中的关键问题。

Method: 提出 EJ-MOEM 多方向边最小修复方法：评估常规模拟六边形广播树方向族，选取容错感知的候选方向，将故障剪枝后的树收缩为健康组件，再利用组件间修复边重新连接这些组件，最终形成健康子图上的有根生成树。

Result: 对于所选方向若故障剪枝组件图连通，则恰好需要 c-1 条外部修复边（c 为健康组件数）；一故障情况下修复深度不超过 t+1，两故障情况下修复深度不超过 t+2。大规模验证（t 达 200）100% 成功。

Conclusion: EJ-MOEM 方法能有效实现密集 EJ 网络中的无冗余广播修复，深度上界定理为网络容错设计提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection networks whose finite quotient geometry is naturally represented by a hexagonal axial-coordinate ball. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense EJ network generated by $α=(t+1)+tω$, where $t$ is the network diameter. We propose EJ-MOEM, a multi-orientation edge-minimum repair method that evaluates a constant-size family of hexagonal broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware candidate, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects these components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph: every healthy node receives the message exactly once, no faulty node is used, and the original healthy tree components are preserved. We prove that, for a chosen orientation whose fault-pruned component graph is connected, exactly $c-1$ external repair edges are necessary and sufficient, where $c$ is the number of healthy components. We also prove a depth-certificate theorem for EJ coordinate-reduction trees: every one-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+1$, and every two-fault placement admits a repair of depth at most $t+2$. The proof uses the three-strip representation of EJ hexagons, a sector-suffix attachment lemma, a non-adjacent-sector separation lemma, and a six-direction shielding classification for paired cuts. Extended validation includes exhaustive one- and two-fault enumeration for $t=2,\ldots,12,14,16,18$ (up to $N=1027$ and 525,825 two-fault placements at $t=18$), structured theorem-critical tests through $t=30$, and large random tests through $t=200$, all with 100\% success and no violation of the theorem.

</details>


### [129] [EVM Workloads in the Wild: Evidence for Multi-Dimensional Gas Metering, State Growth, Delayed Execution, and Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19869)
*Lioba Heimbach,Kushal Babel,Jason Milionis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 通过对2025年以太坊L1和Base L2的追踪级测量研究，发现EVM兼容区块链的Gas计量假设（执行环境稳定、资源组合恒定、状态漂移不影响交易结果）严重失效。Base上46.0%的交易Gas估计随历史状态变化，存储增长永久成本被按瞬时成本定价，Base存储达456GB vs 以太坊38GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证EVM兼容区块链的核心假设——执行条件稳定性和状态无关性——在真实工作量下的失效程度，为多维Gas计量和状态增长显式定价提供经验依据。

Method: 对以太坊L1和Base L2进行2025全年追踪级测量，每日每链采样3000个区块。将交易分解为操作码级执行Gas、固有Gas、退款和持久状态增量。从2025年9月回溯重放交易至历史状态，测量Gas用量和存储访问模式的变化。

Result: 资源组合极不稳定：Base上存储读取和执行计算分别占执行Gas的29.2%和24.3%，以太坊34.9%用于存储写入。以太坊Gas限额翻倍后向计算密集型偏移。Base冷存储读取比例更高（49.7% vs 39.6%）。持久状态增长Base达456GB，以太坊38GB。Base上46.0%交易的Gas估计随历史状态变化，以太坊为13.9%。

Conclusion: 经验数据表明当前单维Gas计量模型不足以捕捉资源组合和状态敏感性的变化，需要多维Gas计量和状态增长的显式定价机制；状态敏感的执行行为使工作量估计复杂化，直接影响交易可预测性和用户体验。

Abstract: Gas metering on EVM-compatible blockchains assumes that execution conditions are stable: that the resource mix is constant enough to justify collapsing execution costs into a single scalar with fixed relative prices, and that state drift between submission and execution does not materially alter a transaction's outcome. We measure the extent to which this assumption fails.
  We present a trace-level measurement study of EVM workloads on Ethereum (L1) and Base (L2) throughout 2025, sampling 3,000 blocks per day per chain. We decompose each transaction into opcode-level execution gas, intrinsic gas, refunds, and persistent state deltas. To measure state sensitivity, we re-execute transactions from September 2025 on older states and record how gas usage and storage access patterns change.
  We find the resource mix to be far from stable: on Base, storage reads and compute account for 29.2% and 24.3% of execution gas, while Ethereum devotes 34.9% to storage writes. Ethereum's gas limit doubling during 2025 shifted its own profile toward compute-heavier, Base-like patterns. Base also exhibits a higher fraction of cold storage reads (49.7% versus 39.6% on Ethereum). Persistent state growth, a permanent cost priced as a transient one, reaches 456 GB on Base versus 38 GB on Ethereum.
  Execution outcomes are equally unstable: gas estimates vary across nearby historical states for 46.0% of transactions on Base, compared to 13.9% on Ethereum, with especially high sensitivity for MEV and DeFi activity. Storage access patterns also diverge across states, limiting the effectiveness of access lists and complicating parallel execution.
  Our work provides an empirical foundation for multi-dimensional gas metering and explicit pricing of state growth. They show that state-sensitive execution behavior complicates workload estimation, directly affecting transaction predictability and user experience.

</details>


### [130] [Online Dynamic Batching with Formal Guarantees for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19989)
*Dian Li,Zekun Wang,Yaoru Wang,Jiahong Yan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出在线动态批处理(ODB)系统，在数据加载阶段利用预处理后可观测的真实样本长度动态构建批次，无需预计算长度缓存或修改模型/优化器，在多种多模态LLM微调场景下实现1.58-4.43x的吞吐量提升，同时保持与固定批次相当的质量，并形式化解决了分布式训练中的组对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练中，样本的真实训练成本（长度）只有在经过预处理、增强、标记化、多模态视觉标记扩展等步骤后才能被观测到，而传统的离线批采样器在批次构建时无法获知这一关键信息，导致填充、内存使用和GPU饱和度的决策是盲目的。虽然有基于令牌预算的批量构建方法，但它们需要昂贵的预计算长度缓存。

Method: ODB是一种DataLoader端即插即用的动态批处理系统，将批次构建推迟到能够准确观测样本长度的时间点（即预处理完成后），同时保持分布式数据并行（DDP）的步骤对齐。论文形式化定义了分布式组对齐问题（DGAP），并证明了在默认join模式身份覆盖和选择性非join样本配额闭合下的无死锁有界终止性。ODB不需要修改模型、优化器或注意力核。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 2B/8B模型上，基于UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o数据集：单节点全量微调/LoRA的吞吐量提升1.58-2.51x，双节点全量微调提升1.71-3.78x，生产环境的MM-Mix达到4.43x。与GMT/BMT离线令牌预算预计算基线相比，在UltraChat/LLaVA上差距在15%以内，在高方差数据集ShareGPT4o上反而快2.24-3.69x。

Conclusion: ODB填补了高异构性LLM微调场景中的在线/即插即用批处理空白：在保持与标准固定批次相当质量的前提下实现大幅吞吐量提升，提供形式化的DGAP保证，无需预计算长度缓存或内核重写。

Abstract: Modern LLM training breaks a core assumption behind offline batch samplers: the true training cost of a sample is only observable after preprocessing, augmentation, templating, tokenization, and multimodal visual-token expansion. Unless one pays for a preprocessing- and augmentation-dependent length cache, batch construction is therefore blind to the quantity that determines padding, memory use, and GPU saturation. We introduce Online Dynamic Batching (ODB), a DataLoader-side drop-in system that moves batch formation to this point of accurate observability while preserving DDP step alignment. We formalize this synchronization requirement as the Distributed Group Alignment Problem and prove deadlock-free bounded termination with default join-mode identity coverage and opt-in non-join sample-quota closure. ODB requires no model, optimizer, or attention-kernel changes and is released as online-dynamic-batching with lightweight trainer adapters. Across public 2B/8B Qwen3-VL runs on UltraChat/LLaVA/ShareGPT4o, ODB improves literal emitted-sample throughput vs. fixed-batch Standard by 1.58-2.51x on single-node Full FT/LoRA and 1.71-3.78x on two-node Full FT, with Standard-comparable quality; production MM-Mix reaches 4.43x. Against GMT/BMT offline token-budget oracles, ODB is within 15% on UltraChat/LLaVA and faster on high-CV ShareGPT4o: 2.24-2.39x single-node Full FT/LoRA and 3.06-3.69x two-node Full FT. Together, ODB occupies the online/drop-in regime for high-heterogeneity LLM fine-tuning: large throughput gains at Standard-comparable quality, formal DGAP guarantees, and no length-cache precompute or kernel rewrites.

</details>


### [131] [ARGUS: Production-Scale Tracing and Performance Diagnosis for over 10,000-GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20374)
*Jiasheng Zhou,Longbin Zeng,Clavis Chen,Ruiming Lu,Qinwei Yang,Leyi Ye,Ray Ying,Key Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ARGUS是一个低开销（<2%）、细粒度、始终开启的追踪和实时分析系统，用于10,000+ GPU规模的生产集群训练工作负载，通过分层观测、数据压缩（约3700倍）和渐进式诊断框架实现持续的性能诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM训练需要始终开启的细粒度可观测性来诊断性能问题，但粗粒度监控无法定位根因，而细粒度分析器开销过高（5%-30%）且产生大量追踪数据，无法在生产集群中始终开启部署。

Method: ARGUS将训练调用层级分解为CPU调用栈、框架语义和GPU内核执行三个维度进行观测；构建统一数据管道，将原始内核事件从每步每rank 10 MB压缩至2.7 KB（约3700倍压缩）；采用渐进式诊断框架，通过迭代时间、阶段级和内核级分析自动隔离异常窗口、掉队rank和性能退化内核。

Result: 在10,000+ GPU生产集群部署超6个月，总开销<2%；成功支持持续的故障慢检（fail-slow detection）和性能优化；案例研究证明了在计算掉队、链路退化、流水线气泡放大、FlashAttention JIT卡顿以及被通信症状掩盖的计算掉队等多种典型异常上的有效性。

Conclusion: ARGUS证明了在超大规模生产集群中实现低开销、始终开启的细粒度可观测性是可行的，能够有效支持训练工作负载的故障检测和性能优化。

Abstract: Large-scale LLM training requires always-on, fine-grained observability for effective performance diagnosis at scale. Coarse resource monitors alone cannot localize root causes, and fine-grained profilers incur prohibitive (5%-30%) overheads and massive trace volumes, making always-on deployment impractical in large production clusters.
  We propose ARGUS, a low-overhead, fine-grained, always-on tracing and real-time analysis system for training workloads in 10,000+ GPU-scale production clusters. ARGUS decomposes observation along the training call hierarchy into CPU call stacks, framework semantics, and GPU kernel execution, with always-on collection under a combined overhead of less than 2%. It builds a unified data pipeline and compresses raw kernel events by approximately 3,700x from 10 MB to 2.7 KB per rank per step. Its progressive diagnosis framework automatically isolates anomalous windows, straggler ranks, and degraded kernels through iteration-time, phase-level, and kernel-level analysis. Deployed for over six months on a 10,000+ GPU production cluster, ARGUS has supported continuous fail-slow detection and performance optimization. Our case studies further demonstrate its effectiveness across representative anomalies, including compute stragglers, link degradation, pipeline-bubble amplification, FlashAttention JIT stalls, and compute stragglers masked by communication symptoms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRei框架，利用基于Rei的道义策略语言（以OWL表示），在LLM驱动的自主AI代理系统中实现超出传统权限控制的完整企业治理，包括义务、豁免、策略冲突解决和本体推理，并通过运行时逻辑引擎强制执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略引擎（如XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅支持允许/禁止的子集，无法满足LLM代理系统所需的完整治理结构，包括义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、豁免和本体推理。

Method: 基于Rei框架构建道义策略语言，以OWL表示策略，在LLM外部由高性能逻辑引擎在运行时评估，同时管控工具调用和代理间消息。与A2AS等行业标准框架自然组合。

Result: 通过示例证明，道义策略能够捕获当前生产引擎大多无法表达的安全和隐私治理约束。

Conclusion: AgenticRei有效填补了自主AI代理系统中企业治理的空白，实现了超越简单权限控制的完整治理功能。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [133] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个人类参与的流水线方法，用于衡量计算机科学本科课程对国际课程指南（CS2013和CS2023）的覆盖程度。通过对某 accredited BSc 项目的纵向分析发现，该项目对两版指南的知识单元覆盖率约为50%，且在十年间基本恒定；但在认知深度上，CS2023的达标率（76%）显著低于CS2013（95%），反映了新指南更高的期望。研究还区分了持续性结构缺陷与指南演进带来的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学本科课程虽受到十年一更新的国际课程指南约束，但缺乏可靠、可复现的方法来量化课程对当前指南的覆盖程度，以及指南结构调整时覆盖情况的变化。

Method: 采用人类参与的流水线：将课程项目和指南分别表示为结构化语料库，通过语义检索生成课程到知识单元的候选匹配，并在明确的覆盖定义下通过人工判断确认匹配。对七个检索器进行基准测试，最终采用倒数排名融合集成。独立第二评分者对两份映射进行验证（Cohen's kappa 0.64-0.69）。

Result: 项目覆盖CS2023的49.7%和CS2013的50.9%知识单元，覆盖率近乎恒定。在覆盖的知识单元中，约88%在两项指南下都表达了胜任力，但CS2023下76%的单元达到推荐认知深度，CS2013下为95%。发现了并行与分布式计算、编程语言基础、系统基础等持续性结构缺陷。

Conclusion: 该流水线是可复用的纵向评估工具，能够区分课程自身的持续性缺陷与指南标准演进带来的差异，为课程改进提供了可量化的依据。

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [134] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对八种现代扩散语言模型（DLMs）在八个基准任务上进行了系统实验分析，评估了生成质量和计算效率，揭示了推理时设计选择（如去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小等）对性能与效率之间权衡的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于已有的扩散语言模型在评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数上存在差异，难以进行公平比较并理解其性能权衡，因此需要系统性的实验分析。

Method: 评估八种最先进的DLMs在八个涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识和结构化问题解决的基准上的表现；分析推理时关键因素（去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小、并行解掩码策略）的影响；并在相同条件下对小模型进行受控对比实验。

Result: DLMs的行为强烈受生成时设计选择影响，导致性能与计算效率之间存在不同的权衡；揭示了不同任务、架构和推理预算下扩散语言建模的优势与局限。

Conclusion: 该研究为当代扩散语言模型的能力和部署特性提供了实用见解，强调了推理时设计选择对模型表现的关键作用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [135] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种封闭循环动力系统模型来解释多智能体LLM deliberation过程，其中每个智能体持有隐藏的“锚点”（内部信念），持续影响其意见。通过分析deliberation动态可以恢复该锚点，并且锚点位置决定了讨论能否突破初始信念的凸包。实验表明，不同模型家族中锚点的影响呈谱系分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究广泛使用多智能体LLM deliberation来提升推理准确性，但缺乏对其工作机制的理论建模。作者试图借鉴人类意见动力学（如DeGroot和Friedkin-Johnsen模型）来解释这一过程，填补多智能体LLM deliberation理论模型的空白。

Method: 将多智能体deliberation建模为封闭循环动力系统，每个智能体携带隐藏的“锚点”（内部信念），该锚点持续拉动其意见（即使受邻居影响）。通过deliberation过程本身可恢复锚点，并检查恢复的锚点是否能预测未参与讨论的轮次（泛化性）来验证模型有效性。

Result: 锚点可以解释经典共识规则所禁止的行为：智能体对正确答案的信心可能超过任何初始值，突破初始信念凸包。三种开放权重模型家族中，锚点的影响强度相似，但锚点位置不同；只有当锚点远离初始意见时，讨论才会突破凸包，需要完整的封闭循环模型来描述。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM deliberation并非简单的共识收敛过程，而是受内部锚点驱动的动态系统。该模型为理解LLM deliberation为何能超越初始信念、提升准确性提供了理论基础，并为判断模型是否真正由锚点驱动提供了可检验的标准。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [136] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DeXposure-Claw系统，利用图时间序列基础模型预测暴露网络，结合确定性监控和应激场景生成监管工单，并通过数据健康和置信度门控减少误报；同时提出DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，在真实五年周度数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融（DeFi）中的信用风险具有快速传播和网络化特征，现有通用LLM代理会过度解读弱证据并推荐高风险干预措施，且缺乏监管对齐的误报评估方法。

Method: (1) DeXposure-FM：图时间序列基础模型预测未来暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控与应激场景将预测转化为类型化警报、归因信号和场景证据；(3) 数据健康与置信度门控约束升级流程，最终生成带有理据的可审计监管工单。评估方面提出DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，决策轴使用监管对齐的绝对损失真实值和显式误干预率对工单打分。

Result: 在五年周度真实数据上的实验完全支持该系统的有效性。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw通过结构化的证据路由机制有效降低了LLM在DeFi监管中的误报率，DeXposure-Bench提供了与监管目标对齐的评估方法。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [137] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了LLM在结构化临床数据任务中识别自身知识局限的能力。通过跨模型归因散度分析，发现LLM的言语化置信度在认知上是空洞的，存在逆难度效应，而SHAP特征证据与少样本示例具有超加性协同作用，且基于归因散度的跨模型校准器能有效替代无信息的言语化置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在结构化临床数据处理中是否能够识别自身知识的局限性，即认知不确定性感知能力，填补该领域的研究空白。

Method: 采用跨模型归因散度分析方法，对比Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在预测任务中的归因差异，通过言语化置信度分析、逆难度效应检验、少样本与SHAP特征干预实验以及基于归因散度的校准器构建，系统评估LLM的认知不确定性。

Result: 1) LLM言语化置信度与预测质量无关，仅在0.856-0.937范围内变化；2) 存在逆难度效应，LLM在XGBoost高度自信时表现差，在中等不确定时表现相当；3) 少样本示例与SHAP特征证据具有超加性效果，将ADS从1.54降至0.38，准确率从49%提升至75.3%；4) 基于归因散度的校准器将期望校准误差从0.254降至0.080。

Conclusion: 将研究发现框架化为LLM在结构化数据上的冷启动问题，并指出实现真正认知自我意识的发展路径。跨模型归因散度可作为无需访问模型内部或重复推理的有效校准信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [138] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续表型相似性建模方法，替代传统离散分组对比学习，在视网膜图像与临床风险叙事的跨模态对齐中实现软监督，显著提升阿尔茨海默病早期预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将表型相似性视为离散构造，依赖硬分组分配，导致监督过于刚性且将分组过程与表示学习解耦，无法捕捉疾病风险的连续谱系特征。

Method: 提出连续表型结构建模：利用模态内嵌入相似度定义可微加权函数，通过连续聚合算子构建软多正样本关系；设计软目标对比学习目标，实现跨模态对齐与表型结构的端到端联合学习。

Result: 在UK Biobank视网膜影像数据上，AD预测任务中持续优于离散分组对比学习及标准视觉-语言基线方法。

Conclusion: 将表型相似性视为可学习的连续信号而非固定分组规则，为视网膜与临床多模态数据的大规模神经退行性疾病风险建模提供了原理性且稳健的基础。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [139] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种让LLM通过自我审视（conscience step）和DPO训练实现伦理对齐的方法，在代码黑客等场景下证明单次高层内省提问即可引导模型走向伦理对齐，无需外部评判模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM能否自主辨别自身输出是否违背人类伦理，以及能否自我纠正，解决模型微调后出现非伦理行为（Emergent Misalignment）的问题。

Method: 在LLM中引入conscience step，使其审视自身推理和输出；使用DPO扩展训练损失中的对齐组件，引导模型远离非伦理输出；依赖冻结的自身副本而非外部评判模型。

Result: 在代码黑客场景下，单次高层内省问题即可实现Emergent Alignment，即模型从微调后的非伦理行为中恢复并走向伦理对齐。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种在线对齐技术，适用于训练、微调、对抗提示和零样本学习等多种场景，无需强弱评判模型，仅依赖模型自身的自我审查机制即可实现伦理对齐。

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [140] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出积分变换网络（ITNet），证明卷积网络、循环网络和Transformer本质上是同一数学对象（可学习积分变换）的特殊情况，并通过统一的架构在图像、语言和3D点云等多个任务上达到或超越专用基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三大架构家族（CNN、RNN、Transformer）各自编码不同的归纳偏置（局部性、序列记忆、成对交互），在数学上长期被割裂看待。作者认为这种割裂并非源于信号处理的本质差异，而是对同一底层数学对象的不完整视角，因此希望构建一个统一的架构。

Method: ITNet基于可学习积分变换，核心是一个依赖位置和特征联合的核函数，由小型MLP实现。通过适当参数化，卷积、自注意力（含多头）和自回归循环（含LSTM、GRU、S4、Mamba）均成为其特例。为实现高效计算，提出平铺核融合、重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分和可学习低秩分解。

Result: 单一ITNet架构（共享算子+轻量级模态特定编码器）在ImageNet-1K、GLUE、ModelNet40、VQA v2和NLVR2上匹敌或超越专用基线。

Conclusion: 一个统一的可学习交互机制能够从数据中恢复所有三种架构家族的行为，表明跨模态信号处理存在统一的数学基础。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [141] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对交互式LLM代理中不确定性表示不足的问题，提出了一种基于提示的分解方法，将行动置信度与请求不确定性（u）分离，使代理能在任务说明模糊时主动请求澄清。在新增的WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification基准上，该方法在五个LLM主干上的澄清F1显著优于ReAct+UE和UAM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典的aleatoric/epistemic不确定性框架不足以支持交互式LLM代理，需要具备欠规范感知、可分解和可通信的不确定性表示，以解锁主动澄清寻求和共享心智模型构建等新能力。同时实际部署约束（黑盒API、交互延迟预算、无标注轨迹）排除了基于logprob、多采样和训练的方法，亟需一种基于提示的可行方案。

Method: 提出一种基于提示的不确定性分解方法，将行动置信度（action confidence）与请求不确定性（request uncertainty, u）分离，使代理在任务说明模糊时主动寻求澄清。为评估该方法，构建两个澄清增强基准（WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification），其中50%的任务故意欠规范。在五个LLM骨干（GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-v3.2-exp、GLM-4.7、Qwen3.5-35B、GPT-OSS-120B）上，与ReAct+UE和UAM进行系统比较。

Result: 在五个LLM骨干上平均，该方法在ALFWorld-Clarification上的澄清F1比ReAct+UE提升73%，比UAM提升36%。在WebShop-Clarification的所有骨干上以及ALFWorld-Clarification的五个骨干中的四个上取得领先，表明增益具有跨模型泛化性。

Conclusion: 基于提示的简单不确定性分解方法能够有效提升LLM代理在欠规范任务中的主动澄清能力，且增益在多个LLM上具有泛化性，为部署友好的不确定性表示提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Recent position papers argue that the classical aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty framework is insufficient for interactive large language model (LLM) agents and call for underspecification-aware, decomposed, and communicable uncertainty representations that can unlock new agent capabilities such as proactive clarification seeking and shared mental-model building. Practical deployment constraints -- black-box APIs, interactive latency budgets, and the absence of labeled trajectories -- rule out logprob-based, multi-sampling, and training-based methods, leaving prompt-based estimation as the most viable family for surfacing such signals at deployment time. We answer this call with a simple prompt-based decomposition that separates action confidence from request uncertainty (u), enabling the agent to ask for clarification when the task specification is ambiguous. To evaluate it, we introduce two clarification-augmented benchmarks (WebShop-Clarification and ALFWorld-Clarification) in which 50% of tasks are deliberately underspecified, and systematically compare the proposed decomposition against ReAct+UE and Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM) across five LLM backbones (GPT-5.1, DeepSeek-v3.2-exp, GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5-35B, GPT-OSS-120B) on these variants together with the standard WebShop, ALFWorld, and REAL benchmarks for fault detection. Averaged across the five backbones, the proposed decomposition improves clarification F1 on ALFWorld-Clarification by 73% over ReAct+UE and by 36% over UAM, and leads clarification F1 on every backbone on WebShop-Clarification and on four of five backbones on ALFWorld-Clarification, indicating that the gains generalize beyond a single LLM.

</details>


### [142] [Analyzing the Narration Gap in LLM-Solver Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19588)
*Zunchen Huang,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM与形式化求解器（SAT/SMT）组成的混合推理管线，发现即便求解器提供可靠的验证结果，但“叙述”环节（将求解器输出转化为用户可读答案）中存在提示注入漏洞，导致最终答案的鲁棒性无法保证。通过形式化建模和五项开源模型的实证评估，论文揭示了证书门控虽能保证求解器结论的可靠性，但攻击者可通过不同措辞和渠道反转已验证结论，而硬化的提示虽有缓解效果但无法彻底消除攻击威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注了LLM-求解器混合管线中的问题形式化（formalization）和决策（decision）环节，但忽略了“叙述”（narration）这一将形式化工具输出转化为用户答案的关键步骤，导致求解器的可靠性保证在最终呈现给用户时可能失效。

Method: 1）对LLM-求解器循环进行形式化建模，将其视为一个可验证的决策过程；2）在五种开源模型上开展提示注入攻击实验；3）分析证书门控机制对求解器结论可靠性的影响；4）评估硬化提示的缓解效果及适应性攻击下的表现。

Result: 证书门控能保证求解器判定的可靠性，但攻击者可通过不同措辞和渠道反转已验证结论；硬化提示能显著降低注入成功率，但无法完全消除威胁，且在适应性攻击下仍然失效。

Conclusion: 在LLM-求解器循环中，鲁棒性无法传递到用户最终读取的答案，叙述环节是安全关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Formal tools such as SAT and SMT solvers are increasingly embedded in language model reasoning pipelines when a safety or security critical question can be formulated in logic. Unlike chain of thought whose steps are sampled from the model distribution without formal guarantee, a solver produces a sound and independently verifiable answer. However, the soundness guarantee can be lost in the interaction between the solver and the model. The hybrid pipeline has three components: formalizing the question, deciding it, and narrating the result. Prior work has studied the formalization and decision, but not narration, which is the step that turns a formal tool's output into the user answer. To fill the narration gap, we first model the LLM-solver loop as a verified decision procedure. We further evaluate five open-sourced models under prompt injection, and we find certificate gating makes the solver verdict sound, while an adversary can invert a verified conclusion across phrasings and channels.
